svāgatenagatam kṛtvā kim karomiti cabravīt
iha puṇyaphalam? ?labdhvā? nadhibandhena? yākṣyase
māṃsaṅkurān apānadau? kurvantiarśāṃsi tānjaguḥ

?ataḥ ?eva icāratavicāritam karma na kartavyam iti

?tebhyaḥ? evabhyanujñātam? tat ?tadā ?madhusūdana

aṣṭāpādyam tu śūdrasya steye bhavati? kilbiṣam
patnī prajāpateḥ ktāā kardamasya tvayānagha?
pṛthivyām? ?pārthivāḥ? ye ca teṣām yūyam parāyaṇam
divasasyaṣṭamam bhagam? muktvā kālatrayam tu yat
yasya yasya ca bībhatsuḥ? vadhe? yatnam kariṣyati
duryodhanina? ca droṇaḥtathuktaḥ kurusaṃsadi
ītavinītena śāstṛeṇa vyavasyanti tathapare

dīyate yatpuṭam vijñāḥtat bhāṇḍapuṭam ucyate
?ūrdhvagāḥ vātaragāḥca vākgrahaḥcadhikaḥ? bhavet?
?ekacakreḥ ?iti ykhyāteḥ? āsīt yaḥtu mahasuraḥ
svatavān pāyau?
manasā cinteyitvāpi yaḥ na datyatpunaḥnaraḥ
?tathā? hi sāmānyātdviguṇam sāram tasmāt aṣṭaguṇam kaliḥ
bhaktyā? praṇāmam rudrasya ye kurvanti dine dine
jāte prakāśe sati ghorarūpaḥ āttaandhakārātapi netṛahīnaḥ
yasmindeśe mṛgaḥ kṛṣṇaḥtasmindharmatnibodhata?
imam tasya mahābhāga? dūtam duṣkarakāriṇaḥ
tam jighāṃsuḥ? aham cāpi teṣām anumate sthitaḥ?
?dvidhāḥ? raṅgaḥ samabhavat? strīṇām dvādham?? ajāyata
siddhayaḥ? nava? sandehaḥ rājīamaṇḍalinām api
tapaḥ cakruḥ ca niyatāḥ prasannaḥ viṣṇuḥ abravīt
yaḥtu matparamam kālam karūtieteṣu mānavaḥ
aśvaḥ tuṣṭraḥ gardabhaḥ? vā
iti ?buddhyāḥ? viniścitya rāghavāya? nyavetayat
aśmamayānām camasānām grahāṇām ca
tuṣeṇāpi aparityaktāḥ ?na prarohanti taṇḍulāḥ
darśanīyam ca śūram ca mādrīputram yudhiṣṭhira??
āvātakṛtbaddhaviṭmūtrāḥ rūkṣā pittakaphe? hitā
vṛṣādinām ca
gurutalpe surapāne steye brāhmaṇahiṃsane
ulyṣmatulyākṛtim dehakṣayavṛtdhikṣayudayam
purāṇam gāruḍam vyāsaḥ purāsau? me abravītidam
tat prayatnena kurvīta viparītam tu varjayet
dadarśayudhajālāni tatheva? kavacāni ca
ṛcaḥ yajūṃṣi sāmāni nirmame yajñasiddhaye?
gaṅgādharaandhakaripuḥ kratudhvaṃsī? vṛṣadhvajaḥ?
buddhiḥ? adhyātmam? ityāhuḥ yathavetanidarśanam

ekadā pitaram ?dṛṣṭvā urudantam aham abruvam
atisthūlasya? bhekasya? mukham? sūtreṇa veṣṭayet
nānāvarṇaḥ bhavet? sūtaḥ vihāya ghanacāpalam
aapaśyatvāmapārśve tu tataṅge padmalakṣaṇam
gotreṇa vai? candramasaḥ nāmnā pramatirucyate
madhurāmlarasam rūkṣam dīpanam pathyadam smṛtam

akṣauhiṇyeva? ?senāyāḥ ?duḥyodhanam? upāgamat

syaāḥcasyabhavan? ?dīrghāḥ? raveḥ aṃśusamaprabhāḥ?
anyebhyaḥ? brāhmaṇāḥ śreṣṭhaḥtebhyaḥcava? kriyāparāḥ
vividhaiḥ?? mṛgasaṅghaiḥca vicitrām citrakananām

jambhakaḥ? kiṅkiṇījālamālamuṣṭram samāsthitaḥ?
upāsate diteḥ putrāḥ sarve labdhavarāḥtathā?
evam uktaḥ ?harivaraḥtadā ?rakṣaḥaṇīśvaram
etatbījam rasāndrasya garbhe? dravati mardanat
lakṣmaṇena ca yutdhāya? buddhim? cakre hi rakṣasām
aśakyā ?tarasā ?jetum pāṇḍavānām anīkinī
kim na brūta ?kumārāḥ ?vaḥ pitaram vai? dvijarṣabham?
evam tu bhojayet? tatra bahūṛcam vedapāragam
navamānat kṛtavatī divasaḥ astam upeyivān
preṣyaḥ aham tava durdharṣa? kriyatām kim pitāmaha
narān eva ?narāḥ ?jaghnuḥ? udagrāḥca ?hayāḥ āhayān
bhīṣmaḥ?
eśrīmārkaṇḍeyeḥ ?uvāca

?yāḥ ?gatiḥ bhavataḥ? sāva? mamāpi sahacāriṇaḥ
bhṛguḥ? uvāca
paraḥ dharmaḥ? kṛtaḥ mahyam śiṣyaḥ asmi bhavatoḥ? sadā
?sāvitreḥ ?iti vikhyātaḥ? praviveśa maharaṇam
andhakārasya? kathitaḥ? deśaḥ nāmnāndhakarakaḥ
ādāḥaśvān?ca kanyām ca viśvāmitram upāgamat

?ṛṣayeḥ ?ūcuḥ

jvalanarkapratīkāśam adhṛṣyam? parvatupamam
śreṣṭham? tatrāpi ca sukhe? tiṣṭhet? siddhāsane sadā
idānīm suhṛtbhidam? śrotum icchāmaḥ?
musalam paraśuḥcakram prāsaḥ daṇḍaṛṣṭitomarāḥ
na ca kāmāt bhayat? krodhat? lobhat? ?vā ?dharmam? utsṛjet
bhaviṣyati? kumāraḥte tejasvī satīavāk iti

atithibhyaḥtu vāṛṇebhyaḥ? deyam śaktyanupūḥvaśaḥ
?śabaraḥ ?uvāca

?yadā utaḥṇasutaḥ garbhān? pāṇḍūnām hanti mādhava?
tatatarhāḥ ?mahātmānaḥ katham? keśava pāṇḍavāḥ
atra bhasma? tat eva
pratyayam me kuruṣvādya yatte manasi rocate

pitṛbhyām? yasya tat dattam tat tasyava? dhanam? bhavet?
uktvanṛtam prakartavyam ?yatināḥ ?dharmalipsunā

kṛtarthaḥ? gaccha? viprandra gaccha? cava? yathāgatam
uṣitāḥca vane kṛcchram? ?yathā? dvādaśa vatsarān
bahurūpasya rājñaḥ hi sūkṣmaḥ apiarthaḥ? na sīdati
iṣāāḥṣṭāḥsamāviṣṭāḥ sarve bhīmaparākramāḥ
?prāṇavityāḥ ?mahavityāḥ ?yaḥ tām vetti sa yogavit
cirasya ?dṛṣṭvā ?rājāndra te anyonyasya priyaṃvade
manuḥ uvāca
punaḥ kāpalikenaktam muñca brāhmaṇam adhvagam?
āsīt ca rājaputrasya ?saḥ ?evayam sumaṅgalaḥ
tayoḥ samabhavat? yuddham? ghorarūpam viśām pate
itiuktvā pāṇḍavam vyāsaḥtatravantaḥadhīyata?
nirviśeṣam ayudhyetām? kṛtapratikṛtaṣiṇau?
yasya vedaḥca vedī ca vitchidyete? tripūruṣam
abhijānāmi? puṣpāṇi tānimāniha lakṣmaṇa
kāmarūpam mahatīrtham? ?kāmākhyāḥ? yatra tiṣṭhati?
śuddhadīpaśikhākāram bhāvayetbhavanaśanam
hriyamāṇe dhane? rājan vayam kasya kṣamemahi
iti kaṣāyaskandhaḥ?
pitrāva? tu ivibhaktāḥ? ye hinadhikasamāḥ? dhanaiḥ??
pathyam? dugdhodanam mudgam vā
vibhaktāḥ? saha jīvantaḥ vibhajeran? punaḥ yadi
paṭargheṇeva? karpāsabījam ?kritvā ?nivasyatām
prayātaḥ dakṣiṇām āśām praviṣṭaḥ kardamam hradam
vadhe? nūnam bhavacśāntiḥtayoḥ vā phalgunasya? vā
vanavāsakṛśām dīnām ?kausalyāḥ? vākyam abravīt
kim te sarvavināśena kṛtena puruṣaṛṣabha?
pipāsaśuṣkahṛtayaḥ praviveśaśramam bhṛgoḥ?
dagdhaḥ? tvayāham avaṭe nyāyena raghunandana
?bhīṣmeḥ? uvāca
putratmṛtān samīkṣyatha? na bhaviṣyanti? mātaraḥ
duryodhanasya? kaṛṇasya śakuneḥ ca durātmanaḥ
janamejayasya rājaṛṣeḥ sa tat atjñasadaḥtadā
bhīmaḥca kekayāḥcava? sātyakiḥca viśām pate
pūrvāhṇe ca pradoṣe ca bhuktamātre prakupyati
agnayaḥca virājamte śastrāṇi kavacāni ca
tenadhunāpi mamaharadāne mandataraḥ
tena me vyākulam cittam hṛdayam dahyativa ca
tvam surasuḥanāthaasi mama ca prapitāmahaḥ
vārṣikān caturaḥ māsān uṣitvā tatra pāṇḍavaḥ
vatsyāmaḥ yatra rājāndra saṃvatsaram imam vayam
pratindhakatkṛtirathaḥ? devamīḍhaḥtatātmajaḥ
svayam śivatanuḥ śiṣyam gaurikarbhagatam smaret
īnaḥhīnaḥ svapatnīāpi tyajyate kim punaḥ parāḥ?
nakasmāt iti jānīmaḥtat sarvam vaktum arhasi
aārāpiudāharantimam itihāsam purātanam
